**§11.4.236 — QA-deploy-readiness gate: no manual-QA hand-off until the mandated validation produced a candidate-fingerprinted PASS verdict; a blocker MUST bind to a seam, never prose (research-derived, 2026-07-31).** Cascade reference (short form): a release artifact MUST NOT be deployed / handed off for manual QA (§11.4.185) until the mandated autonomous validation(s) for THAT artifact produced a machine-written PASS verdict whose artifact fingerprint == the candidate (§11.4.115(F), read FROM THE TARGET at run time); ABSENCE of the verdict BLOCKS the deploy exactly as a FAIL does (§11.4.135) — a not-yet-run validation and a failed one are both refusals, never a silent pass; the refusal lives in the deploy TOOL itself (the seam), never in prose an agent is trusted to honour (§11.4.205/§11.4.226) — a source-gate asserting the validation harness merely EXISTS is necessary-not-sufficient; any requirement an operator declares a blocker (a §11.4.140 `CRITICAL`, or an explicit "release blocker / QA-ready blocker") MUST bind to a mechanical seam at the point it gates, never satisfied by prose and never DOWNGRADED to a follow-up, and when the blocker's own INSTRUMENT bugs the response is §11.4.4/§11.4.120 (STOP, fix the instrument, complete the blocker), never route around it; the seam has NO silent bypass — an override is an explicit, logged, operator-set flag (the §11.4.234/§11.4.135 recorded-deferral pattern); the gate is SELF-VALIDATED (§11.4.107(10)/§11.4.201) with golden-good (verdict present + PASS + fingerprint-match → allow) + golden-bad (absent → refuse / not-pass → refuse / fingerprint-mismatch → refuse) so it cannot itself false-allow, and the verdict-emitter reads the fingerprint from the target at run time, never a hardcoded per-release value (§11.4.111 — a version-pinned validator is the brittleness that lets a stale verdict pass). Honest boundary (§11.4.6): the seam proves a candidate-matching PASS verdict EXISTS for the mandated autonomous validation — it does NOT replace the §11.4.185 manual-QA sufficiency gate (the human check the deploy hands off TO), and it is only as honest as the validation whose verdict it consumes (that validation's own anti-bluff stays §11.4.5/§11.4.69/§11.4.107). Classification: universal (§11.4.17) — consumers supply the mandated-validation set, the verdict path/schema, and the deploy tool per §11.4.35. Composes §11.4.4/.107(10)/.108/.111/.115(F)/.120/.126/.135/.146(D3)/.185/.201/.205/.226/.234. Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-236-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.236`) + recommended gate `CM-QA-DEPLOY-READINESS-GATE` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.236 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--deploy-without-verdict`, `--prose-blocker-suffices`, `--downgrade-blocker-to-followup`, `--skip-readiness-gate`, `--verdict-fingerprint-optional`, `--silent-override` flag.

**§11.4.237 — Mandatory exhaustive context-and-spirit-aware translation review: every localized artifact independently reviewed for CONTEXT (source) + SPIRIT (target-language idiom/register) + technical/non-translatable fidelity — accuracy necessary, never sufficient (operator mandate, 2026-08-05).** Cascade reference (short form): every translation and every translated artifact — UI strings, pages, documents, PDFs, manuals, marketing copy, captions/subtitles, help text, any localized content — MUST undergo a HEAVY, EXHAUSTIVE, INDEPENDENT review by a reviewer (agent or human) STRUCTURALLY SEPARATE from the translator that produced it (§11.4.70/§11.4.20 independence; the translator's own §11.4.92 multi-pass self-review PRECEDES it and NEVER satisfies it, per §11.4.142's no-self-review-substitution discipline applied to localization) — a localized artifact accepted without an independent context-and-spirit review is a §11.4 PASS-bluff at the localization layer. The reviewer holds and applies THREE dimensions: (a) FULL CONTEXT OF THE SOURCE MATERIAL — the original ideas, the subject/domain, the purpose, the intended audience; the reviewer reads the source MEANING, not merely the source STRING, so intent, nuance and tone are carried across, never lost; (b) THE SPIRIT OF THE TARGET LANGUAGE — its tradition, idiom, register, tone and cultural conventions — so the result reads NATIVE, natural, fluent and MAXIMALLY POLISHED, never literal / word-for-word / calqued, a construction correct in the source that reads foreign in the target being REWRITTEN into the natural target-language form; (c) CORRECT TECHNICAL VOCABULARY AND VERBATIM PRESERVATION OF NON-TRANSLATABLE TERMS — domain/technical terms rendered with their established target-language equivalents, and non-translatable tokens (product names, brand names, proper nouns, technology/library/API names, code identifiers, trademarks) preserved VERBATIM, never "translated", never accidentally transliterated, never localized away. The goal is a slick, polished, professional, native-reading localization: fidelity to the source is the FLOOR (accuracy NECESSARY), never the ceiling (accuracy NEVER SUFFICIENT) — a rendering that is accurate but stilted / foreign / register-wrong / idiom-broken is a FINDING, not a pass. Anti-bluff: the review emits captured, machine-readable POSITIVE evidence per §11.4.5/§11.4.69 (`feature_class` = translation_review) — a per-artifact verdict carrying a PER-DIMENSION rationale across the closed dimension set {fidelity, fluency/naturalness, spirit/register, terminology, completeness, non-translatable-preservation}; a PASS with no captured per-dimension rationale is a §11.4/§11.4.1 bluff, and a FAIL on ANY dimension LOOPS BACK to re-translation/polish and re-review, iterating to a zero-finding clean verdict per §11.4.134 (a single pass that "looked fine" never closes the artifact). Honest boundary (§11.4.6): this anchor REFINES the existing localization discipline from PASS/FAIL accuracy checking to CONTEXT-AND-SPIRIT native-polish review — it does NOT replace §11.4.140's canonical translation pipeline nor §11.4.141's independent per-language review (a KNOWN operator-owned §11.4.54 anchor-number collision on `11.4.140`/`11.4.141`, cited here strictly in those senses, re-mint still operator-owned, introducing no new collision), it STRENGTHENS them (accuracy → native polish); it does not make a mistranslation impossible (a miscalibrated reviewer stays §11.4.107(10) golden-good/golden-bad fixture territory), and where a target language genuinely has no established term for a source concept that gap is an honestly recorded finding (§11.4.3 SKIP-with-reason), never a silently invented word. Classification: universal (§11.4.17) — no vendor/project/language literal; consumers supply their language set, term glossary, non-translatable token list, and reviewer substrate as DATA per §11.4.35. Composes §11.4.140/.141/.6/.69/.92/.134/.142/.209/§1.1. Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-237-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.237`) + recommended gate `CM-TRANSLATION-CONTEXT-SPIRIT-REVIEW` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.237 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--skip-spirit-review`, `--accuracy-only-OK`, `--self-review-translation-OK`, `--literal-translation-OK`, `--translate-non-translatable-OK`, `--skip-per-dimension-rationale` flag.

**§11.4.238 — Automated QA must be the DISCOVERER: manual QA finds nothing new, and anything it finds is a coverage escape (User mandate, 2026-08-08).** Verbatim operator mandate: *"Helix QA full websites testing MUST find all such issues, not us! Once we do manual QA we shall not be able to find anything! THIS IS MANDATORY RULE / CONSTRAIN / GOAL to achieve with this and any other project and MUST BE part of the root constitution!"* Forensic case study (FACT, 2026-08-08): in one cycle five real defects were found — a health endpoint returning `{"status":"healthy","components":[]}` that was structurally incapable of ever reporting unhealthy; a gateway routing every completion at a port nothing served (HTTP 500 on the product's primary capability); two submodule pins on `main` that upstream answers with `not our ref`, so no fresh clone could check them out; and two hardware-profiler defects reading a key from a file where it does not exist. **Every one was found by an agent reading source or probing by hand; not one by the automated QA system**, which reported green throughout. The mandate (ALL hold): **(A) the automated QA system is the DISCOVERER, not the confirmer** — discovery by a human, an operator's manual pass, an agent reading code, or an end-user report is a FAILURE of the QA system; the finding is still fixed, but the QA gap it exposes is a defect of equal standing and closing only the reported defect is the violation. **(B) manual QA must find NOTHING NEW** — by the time manual QA runs (§11.4.185) the automated regime must already have surfaced everything, so manual QA is a CONFIRMATION and sufficiency gate, never the discovery mechanism; the target is explicit and measurable — a manual pass discovering zero previously-unknown defects. **(C) every escape triggers the §11.4.138 loop, widened** to EVERY out-of-band channel: systematic-debug the defect (§11.4.102), produce a **coverage-escape audit** naming WHY the regime missed it cited to the specific missing check / unexercised path / absent assertion (never "we didn't think of it"), register a new or strengthened automated check that WOULD have caught it into the standing regime (§11.4.135) with a §11.4.115 RED capturing the escaped defect, and commit the audit as captured evidence. **(D) coverage is measured by what the regime can CATCH, not what it runs** — every surface falsifiable per §1.1 (a check whose paired mutation does not make it fail is decoration), exercising real user-reachable paths end-to-end (§11.4.98 / §11.4.143 / §11.4.136 / §11.4.158 / §11.4.159 / §11.4.160). **(E) directional and measured** — track the discovery-channel split (automated vs out-of-band) and drive out-of-band toward zero; honest boundary (§11.4.6): this does NOT claim a regime can be provably complete (§11.4.118) and does NOT license closing an escape by narrowing the definition of "defect". Composes §11.4.27 / §11.4.138 (trigger set widened) / §11.4.185 (manual QA becomes confirmation, never discovery) / §11.4.118 / §11.4.135 / §11.4.115 / §11.4.98 / §11.4.136 / §11.4.143 / §11.4.158 / §11.4.159 / §11.4.160 / §11.4.102 / §11.4.6 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17) — the consuming project supplies its QA system, surface inventory, and discovery-channel ledger per §11.4.35. Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-238-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.238`) + recommended gates `CM-QA-IS-THE-DISCOVERER` / `CM-MANUAL-QA-FINDS-NOTHING-NEW` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227).

**Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.238. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--manual-qa-may-discover`, `--skip-coverage-escape-audit`, `--found-by-human-is-fine`, `--close-escape-without-new-check`, `--qa-green-is-enough`, `--discovery-channel-not-tracked` flag.

## CONST-051(A) — Project instantiation: the concrete equal-codebase roster (§11.4.35)

> **Operator mandate (2026-09-02):** *"Make sure that all of our systems, sub-systems,
> submodules and everything we control in our organizations repos is treated equally as
> part of the project — work done on them all equally as on main repo module itself!
> Especially Helix Agent, LLM, Translate, QA, etc. Make sure that this fact is part of
> the local project's constitution so it is absolutely clear."*

The universal rule already exists: **CONST-051(A) / §11.4.28(A) equal-codebase**. This
section is its **§11.4.35 project instantiation** — the concrete roster the universal
rule requires the consuming project to supply, so "every owned submodule" is never an
abstraction anyone has to guess at.

### The rule, stated without hedging

Every repository listed below is **an equal part of HelixCode's codebase — not a
dependency, not a vendored blob, not somebody else's problem.** Work performed on any
of them carries **exactly** the same obligations as work on the main module:

- the same anti-bluff posture — every PASS carries captured runtime evidence (§11.4, §11.4.5, §11.4.123)
- the same test-type floor (§11.4.169) and four-layer coverage (§11.4.4(b))
- the same TDD discipline and coverage floor (§11.4.224)
- the same independent code review, iterated to a zero-finding GO (§11.4.125 / §11.4.134 / §11.4.142)
- the same documentation, user manuals and guides, kept in sync (§11.4.12 / §11.4.65)
- the same Challenges + HelixQA coverage (CONST-050(B))
- the same gates and paired §1.1 mutations
- the same release discipline — prefixed tags (§11.4.151), ff-only pushes, never force (§11.4.113)

**A round of work that improves the main module while leaving an owned submodule's
equivalent deficiency unaddressed is a CONST-051(A) violation**, regardless of how good
the main-module work was. Coverage ledgers (§11.4.48) list every repository below as
in-scope.

### The roster — 118 owned repositories of 131 total (measured 2026-09-02)

**HelixDevelopment (13)** — the Helix family the operator named explicitly:

| Path | Repository |
|---|---|
| `submodules/helix_agent` | `HelixDevelopment/HelixAgent` |
| `submodules/helix_llm` | `HelixDevelopment/HelixLLM` |
| `submodules/helix_qa` | `HelixDevelopment/HelixQA` |
| `submodules/helix_memory` | `HelixDevelopment/HelixMemory` |
| `submodules/helix_specifier` | `HelixDevelopment/HelixSpecifier` |
| `submodules/doc_processor` | `HelixDevelopment/DocProcessor` |
| `submodules/llm_orchestrator` | `HelixDevelopment/LLMOrchestrator` |
| `submodules/llm_provider` | `HelixDevelopment/LLMProvider` |
| `submodules/vision_engine` | `HelixDevelopment/VisionEngine` |
| `submodules/debate_orchestrator` | `HelixDevelopment/DebateOrchestrator` |
| `submodules/dag_orchestrator` | `HelixDevelopment/DagOrchestrator` |
| `submodules/pipeline_runtime` | `HelixDevelopment/PipelineRuntime` |
| `constitution` | `HelixDevelopment/constitution` |

**HelixDevelopment-Code (1):** `github_pages_website` → `HelixDevelopment-Code/Welcome`

**vasic-digital (104):** every submodule whose URL is `git@github.com:vasic-digital/*`
— the `submodules/*` capability modules (containers, security, challenges, llms_verifier,
storage, auth, database, cache, rag, memory, agentic, …) and the `cli_agents/caf-*`
fork family. Enumerate the live list with:

```bash
grep -E '^\s*(path|url)' .gitmodules | paste - - | grep -E 'vasic-digital|HelixDevelopment'
```

**Not on the roster — 13 third-party submodules are READ-ONLY.** Any repository whose
URL owner is neither `vasic-digital` nor `HelixDevelopment*` (llama.cpp, ollama,
huggingface_hub, docling, skyvern, mcp servers, the `cli_agents_resources/*` reference
corpora, …) is vendored: we consume it, we do not modify it, and it is pinned at an
upstream revision. Local modifications to these are drift to be reverted, never work to
be committed.

### Honest boundary (§11.4.6)

The operator's mandate named **"Helix Agent, LLM, Translate, QA"**. Agent, LLM and QA
are on the roster above and verified present. **There is no `helix_translate` submodule
in this project** — `grep helix_translate .gitmodules` returns 0 matches, and the only
occurrence of that name anywhere in the governance corpus is as an illustrative example
string inside the §11.4.151 release-prefix rule (`helix_translate-1.0.0-dev-0.0.1`).
It is recorded here as **NOT INCORPORATED** rather than silently listed as though it
existed. If Helix Translate is a real repository that belongs in this project, adding it
is a §11.4.74 catalogue-check + submodule-incorporation task, and this roster must be
updated in the same commit.

**Roster maintenance:** this list is data, and it drifts. Any commit that adds or removes
an owned submodule MUST update this section in the same commit — a stale roster
under-states the equal-codebase surface and is itself a CONST-051(A) defect.

above and were not touched by this backfill.

**§11.4.239 — Critical-invariant work-class Definition of Done mandate — money/safety/availability/integrity code MUST include failure-path scenarios as gates, not merely happy-path passes (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): every project's Definition of Done (§11.4.15/§11.4.16 lifecycle + §11.4.33 closure vocabulary) MUST identify a closed CRITICAL-INVARIANT WORK CLASS — code paths where a single incorrect outcome is unrecoverable in the window between failure and remediation — and MUST require that a change touching that class carry EXPLICIT FAILURE-PATH SCENARIOS turned into passing tests BEFORE closure; a DoD stopping at "code compiles + happy-path test green" on such code is "a machine for generating reopened tickets on the paths that move money". ALL hold: **(A)** four universal categories, consumer-extended as DATA per §11.4.35 — MONEY (credit/debit/transfer/hold/release/refund/reconcile), SAFETY (outputs actuating hardware / medical / transport / industrial controls), AVAILABILITY (the primary user-facing capability's critical path), INTEGRITY (mutations of a persistent record whose corruption cannot be silently repaired); a region is IN the class when at least one category applies, and the four are NOT claimed exhaustive for every domain. **(B)** failure paths are FIRST-CLASS acceptance criteria enumerated BEFORE the code is authored, each a Given/When/Then scenario whose "Then" observes a real user-observable outcome (a ledger row, a settled charge, a state delta) rather than "no error" (§11.4.69); the reference MONEY minimum is FRESH / IDEMPOTENT RETRY / SAME-KEY-DIFFERENT-PAYLOAD / CRASH-IN-THE-MIDDLE (canon: "the two criteria juniors omit are same-key-different-payload … and the crash-in-the-middle"), with analogous consumer-supplied minima for SAFETY, AVAILABILITY and INTEGRITY; an omitted scenario is a finding of §11.4/§11.4.1 severity, never a "we'll add it if it bites us" deferral. **(C)** the precondition binds MECHANICALLY through the existing §11.4.146(D3) status-custody chain — a done/ready/closed-status write against an item whose diff touches the class is REFUSED unless the registry row cites coverage of the declared scenario set, each scenario mapped to a §11.4.115(F) machine-written verdict pair carrying §11.4.226 class-matched evidence; an "N/A" scenario MUST cite a §11.4.6-honest reason, a bare "N/A" is treated as omission. **(D)** composes §11.4.108 (scenarios close on runtime-class evidence, never source-green), §11.4.129 (huge-blocker protocol governs a scenario-surfaced defect during release validation), §11.4.185 (manual QA un-weakened — automation NECESSARY never sufficient) and §11.4.238 (a manual-QA-discovered failure-path defect is BOTH a coverage-escape audit and an acceptance-criteria audit). **(E)** honest boundary (§11.4.6) — DIRECTIONAL: it forbids the "happy path suffices" shape and mandates a domain-appropriate minimum, it does NOT claim exhaustive enumeration of every failure mode (§11.4.118 discovery-pressure territory); each escape becomes a permanent addition to the scenario set (§11.4.135). Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-239-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.239`) + recommended gate `CM-CRITICAL-INVARIANT-FAILURE-PATH-DOD` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.239 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--happy-path-suffices-for-money`, `--skip-failure-scenarios`, `--defer-failure-path-tests`, `--criticality-not-applicable-without-reason`, `--n/a-without-justification`, `--closure-without-scenario-map` flag.

**§11.4.240 — Producer ≠ Verifier scope separation: the actor that produces a change/artifact/verdict cannot be the one that gates it — least privilege over cleverness (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): every project MUST enforce STRUCTURAL SEPARATION between the actor that PRODUCES a change/artifact/verdict and the actor that GATES it — the producer cannot write, weaken, delete or edit the acceptance tests, the oracle, the gate configuration, the verdict store, the paired §1.1 mutation, or any other check whose green result certifies the producer's own work. ALL hold: **(A)** FOUR SCOPES that MUST resolve to distinct actors — PRODUCER (writes the diff/config/migration/artifact code), ORACLE (decides "correct output" per §11.4.245), GATE (decides pass/fail — CI config, gate scripts, §11.4.146(D3) custody enforcer, §11.4.135 release-verdict machinery), VERIFIER (re-runs the gate from a CLEAN CHECKOUT and attaches receipts per §11.4.165/§11.4.209/§11.4.194); canon: "the agent that wrote the code cannot be the authority that certifies it — self-preference bias and shared context make self-review worthless … Remove any one property and it degrades to self-certification"; a four-scope collapse is maximum-severity, any two-scope collapse involving the producer is a finding of §11.4/§11.4.1 severity. **(B)** enforcement is by CAPABILITY, not INSTRUCTION — "least privilege over cleverness … if it can't touch the gate, it can't game it": filesystem scope excluding `tests/`/`oracle/`/CI-config/gate/verdict paths, branch + review protection (§11.4.248 PROTECTED-SPEC + CODEOWNERS), verdict-store isolation (§11.4.115(F) harness writes, never the producer's code path), and execution isolation (fresh context, clean checkout, no inherited dirty worktree); "we told it in the prompt not to hardcode" is canon's named shallow/red-flag answer — exhortation is refused as the enforcement layer. **(C)** THREE FORBIDDEN COLLAPSES — PRODUCER=ORACLE (tautological testing; countered by authoring the oracle from spec / §11.4.243 characterization baseline / §11.4.245 derived reference), PRODUCER=GATE (silenced gates / weakened thresholds; countered by §11.4.248-protected paths + a non-producer approver), PRODUCER=VERIFIER (self-certification; countered by §11.4.115(F) machine-written fingerprint-keyed verdicts + the §11.4.135 release seam refusing producer-asserted verdicts). **(D)** composes the existing verifier layer (§11.4.115(F)/§11.4.125/§11.4.135/§11.4.142/§11.4.146(D3)/§11.4.165/§11.4.194/§11.4.209) — §11.4.240 ARTICULATES the property those mechanisms enforce and forbids the collapses that would make them decoration. **(E)** honest boundary (§11.4.6) — separation forbids the CLASS of self-certification; it does NOT prove the verifier correct (§11.4.107(10)), nor the oracle correct (§11.4.245), nor close every gaming vector (over-fitting to seen cases stays §11.4.245 held-out/property/metamorphic territory). **(F) INDEPENDENCE-TIER VOCABULARY + TIER-C TRIGGERS, DEGRADING HONESTLY (extension, 2026-08-26)** — every accepted verdict records its achieved basis of independence from the CLOSED SET `{instance, model, capability}`; model diversity is PERMITTED, NEVER REQUIRED (refusing a same-model independent verdict at an ordinary seam is itself the §11.4.201 false-positive-refusal class), and this does NOT weaken §11.4.256's different-model translation-review pin nor §11.4.209's Fable-`xhigh` code-review pin; Tier-C strength is triggered by (1) the producer holding write access to the evidence/verdict store, or (2) the `release-tag` / `qa-deploy` / `manual-qa-handoff` seams (where a producer-authored verdict is REFUSED regardless); outside those two triggers a separate agent INSTANCE MUST SUFFICE and capability separation MUST NOT be demanded; on a SINGLE-UID host capability separation cannot be established by any mechanism an agent can set up, so such a host reports `instance` HONESTLY and never `capability` — the field records what was ACHIEVED, never what was ASPIRED to, and honest degradation IS the compliant path. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-240-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.240`) + recommended gates `CM-PRODUCER-NOT-VERIFIER-SCOPE-SPLIT` / `CM-LEAST-PRIVILEGE-CAPABILITY-NOT-INSTRUCTION` / `CM-INDEPENDENCE-TIER-HONESTLY-RECORDED` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.240 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--producer-may-write-tests`, `--self-certification-OK`, `--exhortation-suffices-as-enforcement`, `--producer-authored-verdict-accepted`, `--capability-limit-optional`, `--policy-document-is-enforcement`, `--single-actor-for-all-scopes` flag.

**§11.4.241 — Illegal-state-unrepresentability preference: types before API-shape before lint before property-test before runtime-assertion (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): every load-bearing invariant a change relies on MUST land at the STRONGEST enforcement rung the target language/toolchain supports, on the closed ordered ladder (1) TYPE SYSTEM → (2) API SHAPE → (3) LINT → (4) PROPERTY-BASED TEST → (5) RUNTIME ASSERTION; a defect PREVENTABLE at rung N but caught only at rung > N is a finding. Rung-by-language capability baseline: strongly-typed languages reach rungs 1–5; dynamically-typed with gradual typing reach rung 1 conditionally; untyped scripting reaches rungs 3–5 unconditionally. Genuinely-limited-environment clarification: a POSIX-sh surface with only rung 5 SATISFIES the anchor IFF the runtime assertion GENUINELY CATCHES the invariant AND is paired with its §1.1 meta-test mutation per §11.4.107(10) self-validation. Composes §11.4.108 (pushes enforcement LEFT of Layer 1) / §11.4.115 / §11.4.142 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 / §11.4.224 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.35 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-241-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.241`) + recommended gate `CM-ILLEGAL-STATE-LADDER-PREFERENCE` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.241 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35); the canonical block is pointer-plus-summary form naming a `curriculum_anchors_241_254_full_drafts.md` draft NOT present in this checkout, so this reference derives strictly from the in-canon summary (§11.4.6). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--runtime-assert-suffices`, `--skip-type-preference`, `--dynamic-language-exempts-all-rungs`, `--property-test-in-lieu-of-type`, `--decorative-type-annotation-OK` flag.

**§11.4.242 — Bisection-not-blame regression cause-finding + failure modes (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): when a previously-working feature/behaviour regresses, the FIRST-choice localisation mechanism is BISECTION (`git bisect` or an equivalent monotone search, O(log N)) over the ordered commit range between the last known-good tag (§11.4.114) and current HEAD — NOT blame-log inspection, NOT speculative root-cause hunting. MONOTONE-SEARCH INVARIANT: bisection is valid only when the observable is monotone across the range (broken stays broken); a non-monotone observable (flaky, environment-dependent, timing-race) MUST be stabilised first, because bisection over a flaky signal produces a WRONG "guilty" commit with the same confidence as a correct one (a §11.4/§11.4.1 bluff at the localisation layer). CLOSED 6-CLASS FAILURE-MODE CATALOGUE: (1) flaky/non-monotone observable; (2) build-broken commits mistaken for "bad" (use `bisect skip`, exit 125); (3) merge-commit introductions (the defect is in the conflict resolution, in neither parent); (4) external/environmental changes; (5) delayed-fuse introductions (latent commit + later trigger); (6) test-infra regressions masquerading as product regressions. A `git bisect run` script MUST honour exit-code discipline (0=good, 1–124=bad, 125=skip, 128+=abort) — mapping every non-zero to "bad" collapses the taxonomy. The bisection RESULT is captured evidence (§11.4.5/§11.4.69) feeding §11.4.102's root-cause phase. **EXTENSION — DELTA DEBUGGING (2026-08-20):** `git bisect` IS delta debugging over commits, not the reverse; when a failure is not a monotone commit-range regression, or when a commit-bisection returns a change too large to explain it, the investigation MUST narrow along the discriminating axis — input, config, data, dependency or feature flag — to a MINIMAL failing case that becomes the §11.4.115 RED test's input, under "a stable, fast, unambiguous test predicate", running the predicate multiple times per step where the failure is noisy (§11.4.50/§11.4.248); minimisation yields the smallest input that still triggers the failure, which canon says "usually is the diagnosis", NOT the root cause (that stays §11.4.102). Honest boundary (§11.4.6): bisection localises the INTRODUCING COMMIT of a MONOTONE regression; it does NOT prove that commit's change is the ROOT CAUSE. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-242-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.242`) + recommended gates `CM-BISECTION-BEFORE-BLAME` / `CM-DELTA-DEBUG-MINIMAL-CASE` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.242 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--blame-log-suffices`, `--skip-bisection`, `--flaky-observable-bisect-anyway`, `--run-script-exit-code-collapse-OK`, `--regression-guess-without-localisation` flag.

**§11.4.243 — Characterization / golden-master safety net for legacy code before behaviour-changing modification (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): before ANY behaviour-changing modification to legacy code lacking characterization tests, the modifier MUST FIRST author CHARACTERIZATION TESTS asserting the CURRENT observable behaviour as GROUND TRUTH (whether "correct" or not) and land them as a green baseline; only AFTER the safety net exists may the modification proceed. The pattern originates in Feathers's WORKING EFFECTIVELY WITH LEGACY CODE and generalises GOLDEN-MASTER (record outputs across a representative input space; assert future runs match). LEGACY SCOPE (§11.4.6-honest, any one suffices): (i) lacks tests asserting observable behaviour; (ii) written spec diverges from measured actual behaviour; (iii) predates the constitution's TDD discipline (§11.4.224); (iv) the modifier cannot describe its exact behaviour from reading source within the time budget. AUTHORING LOOP: identify the observable surface → generate a representative input space → run and record actual outputs as the ground-truth baseline → assert the recorded values → commit the characterization tests as a SEPARATE commit BEFORE the behaviour-changing modification. DISTINCTION FROM CORRECTNESS: characterization is DESCRIPTIVE not prescriptive — it captures WHAT IS, not WHAT SHOULD BE; where current behaviour is subtly wrong the test CAPTURES that wrongness and the modifier MUST notice and ASK the operator per §11.4.66 whether to preserve or correct it, never decide silently. Composes §11.4.115 (RED-first — characterization is its legacy counterpart) / §11.4.124 (investigate-before-remove, the parallel discipline) / §11.4.118 (a sampled input space is an honest gap, not completeness) / §11.4.201 / §11.4.224. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-243-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.243`) + recommended gate `CM-CHARACTERIZATION-BEFORE-LEGACY-MODIFICATION` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.243 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--modify-untested-legacy-directly`, `--skip-characterization`, `--claim-tests-not-needed-because-obvious`, `--decorative-assertion-suffices`, `--refactor-without-safety-net` flag.

**§11.4.244 — Cross-boundary contract tests + can-i-deploy gate (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): a change modifying a boundary between independently-deployable components (service↔consumer, library↔dependents, DB schema↔readers/writers, message topic↔subscribers) MUST be covered by CONSUMER-DRIVEN CONTRACT TESTS on BOTH sides, AND a `can-i-deploy` gate MUST verify the proposed provider version's compatibility against every currently-deployed consumer version BEFORE the change ships. §11.4.244 deliberately MERGES the two — they are useless in isolation: contract tests without the gate produce compatibility knowledge nobody consults, and a can-i-deploy gate without contract tests has nothing to check. CDC pattern: consumers publish the assumptions they make about the provider (Pact / Spring Cloud Contract / gRPC schema-evolution rules); providers verify each consumer's contract against the actual implementation; a provider change breaking a live consumer's contract FAILS THE PROVIDER'S BUILD before the change ships — "a precise red at the source". CAN-I-DEPLOY: at every deploy attempt the gate queries the contract broker for the compatibility matrix; a single red cell BLOCKS the deploy with a machine-readable verdict naming the incompatible pair, NEVER warning-with-continue; a MISSING contract is a first-class refusal reason per §11.4.201 (a false-positive REFUSAL is a FAIL-bluff exactly as a false-negative allowance is a PASS-bluff). SemVer becomes MEASURABLE — a PATCH bump that breaks a contract is a mis-labelled version. The backward-compatibility window is declared per §11.4.35. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.66 / §11.4.108 (per-side four-layer) / §11.4.135 (contract tests as permanent regression guards) / §11.4.185 (composed-system manual QA still needed) / §11.4.201 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-244-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.244`) + recommended gates `CM-CONTRACT-TESTS-BOTH-SIDES` / `CM-CAN-I-DEPLOY-GATE-WIRED` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.244 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--skip-contract-tests`, `--deploy-without-compatibility-check`, `--assume-compatible-when-broker-silent`, `--patch-may-break-contract`, `--contract-tests-optional-for-internal-boundaries` flag.

**§11.4.245 — Oracle-problem-first test authoring: identify the oracle BEFORE authoring the test (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): every test MUST FIRST identify its ORACLE — the mechanism deciding PASS vs FAIL for the input it drives — and that oracle MUST be DIFFERENT from the code under test AND from the test's own assertion mechanism; authoring without explicit oracle identification produces the "test agrees with the code" anti-pattern (the test asserts what the code happens to produce, so it passes as long as the code produces something and never catches wrong-output — the most common form of §11.4/§11.4.1 bluff at the test-authoring layer). §11.4.245 does not SOLVE the oracle problem (the oldest unsolved problem in software testing); it mandates HONESTLY IDENTIFYING which oracle strategy each test uses so the §11.4.194 reviewer can judge whether that oracle is genuinely independent. CLOSED ORACLE-STRATEGY SET, named explicitly in each test's source — (1) SPECIFIED (a spec/RFC/standard names the expected output); (2) DERIVED (an independent reference implementation); (3) METAMORPHIC (relations between related inputs — §11.4.107(8) generalised); (4) GOLDEN-MASTER / CHARACTERIZATION (past captured behaviour — §11.4.243); (5) INVARIANT (a universal property that must hold — sort output sorted AND a permutation of the input); (6) STATISTICAL (distribution properties for stochastic outputs, never a specific value); (7) HUMAN (§11.4.185 manual QA). NO other classes. STRUCTURAL INDEPENDENCE: the oracle's SOURCE of the expected value must not depend on the code being tested. §11.4.107(10) SELF-VALIDATION generalised: the paired §1.1 mutation must produce a WRONG output and the oracle must catch it. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.107(10) / §11.4.115 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 / §11.4.224 / §11.4.243 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-245-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.245`) + recommended gate `CM-ORACLE-STRATEGY-NAMED-AND-INDEPENDENT` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.245 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--test-agrees-with-code`, `--skip-oracle-identification`, `--oracle-is-the-code`, `--assertion-is-oracle`, `--implicit-oracle-OK` flag.

**§11.4.246 — Reproducible + hermetic builds + supply-chain integrity at SLSA Build Level 2 (operator decision, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): every consuming project's build pipeline MUST produce REPRODUCIBLE, HERMETIC artifacts and MUST meet SLSA Build Level 2 as the FLEET-WIDE MINIMUM (operator decision 2026-08-15; Level 3+ tracked as backlog per §11.4.197). REPRODUCIBLE = the same source at the same time on any conformant host produces byte-identical artifacts, or a diff-set fully accounted for by declared non-determinisms pinned to canonical values. HERMETIC = no undeclared inputs — every dependency version-pinned and lockfile-hashed, every tool version declared, no unpinned public-network reach during the build, no host-environment leakage. SLSA-L2 = signed provenance describing source URI + commit hash + builder identity + invocation + artifact hash, produced on a hosted tamper-resistant build platform (the project's ancillary posture is already satisfied by SSH-only remotes §2.1, rootless podman §11.4.161, fixed-tag pins §11.4.30/§11.4.77). Every consumer MAINTAINS `docs/security/SLSA_LEVEL.md` (or its §11.4.35 equivalent) recording the CURRENT level with cited evidence, the TARGET level, and tracked §11.4.197 upgrade items — a CURRENT below L2 with no tracked item is a violation. SUPPLY-CHAIN EXTENSION: dependencies are either vendored (checked in, hash-verified), sourced from a provenance-attested registry, OR mirrored through a hash-pinning local registry. THREAT MODEL IS NARROW: it covers the source→artifact chain; it does NOT cover a malicious commit landing in source (§11.4.142 review), a compromised dependency source, or a bug-free artifact (§11.4.108 four-layer + §11.4.185 manual QA). Composes §2.1 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.30 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.108 / §11.4.113 / §11.4.135 / §11.4.142 / §11.4.161 / §11.4.184 / §11.4.185 / §11.4.197 / §11.4.209 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-246-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.246`) + recommended gates `CM-BUILD-REPRODUCIBILITY` / `CM-BUILD-HERMETICITY` / `CM-SLSA-L2-PROVENANCE-VERIFIED` / `CM-SUPPLY-CHAIN-DEPS-PROVENANCE-ATTESTED` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.246 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--skip-reproducibility`, `--allow-network-during-build`, `--no-provenance`, `--slsa-optional`, `--below-l2-OK`, `--unpinned-dependency-OK`, `--host-env-may-leak-into-build` flag.

**§11.4.247 — Composite-output layer-move must move every layer (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): a change MOVING a system-boundary layer (a service migrating hosts, an endpoint URL change, an API version bump, a DB schema shift, a message-topic rename, an ingress path re-route, a storage-bucket move) MUST move EVERY LAYER of the composite output atomically or via an explicit compatibility window — NEVER piecemeal. LAYER TAXONOMY (§11.4.35 baseline, consumer-supplied concrete inventory): (i) server (handler + route + OpenAPI/proto/gRPC descriptor); (ii) client (call-sites + SDK stubs + integration tests); (iii) infrastructure (load balancer + ingress + API gateway + CDN + service mesh + DNS); (iv) data stores (cached keys + DB URL rows + MQ subscriptions); (v) observability (metric labels + span names + log fields + dashboards + alerts + SLOs); (vi) documentation (user + dev + runbook + README + cookbook); (vii) tests (integration + e2e + contract per §11.4.244 + load + smoke). The consuming project provides a `scripts/audit/layer_move_completeness.sh`-class residual-reference report; a change proposing a move MUST show ZERO residuals OR an explicit compatibility-window plan per residual with tracked §11.4.197 items. COMPATIBILITY WINDOW: the old value keeps working alongside the new until a stated date/version/event, deprecation warnings are named per caller, the deadline is tracked, and removal at expiry is clean — never "clean up later". Canon records a MATERIAL-THIN + CITATION-AUDIT note: the phrases "composite-output", "surface-swap-not-recreate" and "atomic move discipline" have ZERO occurrences in the tracked corpus and are this anchor's OWN PARAPHRASE, not quoted source text; the module-32 DOMAIN attribution is plausible and unrefuted while the PHRASE-level attribution is UNVERIFIED (§11.4.6). Composes §11.4.6 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.111 / §11.4.118 / §11.4.124 / §11.4.135 / §11.4.191 / §11.4.197 / §11.4.238 (a residual IS a coverage escape) / §11.4.244 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-247-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.247`) + recommended gate `CM-LAYER-MOVE-COMPLETENESS-CHECK` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.247 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--partial-layer-move-OK`, `--skip-completeness-check`, `--residuals-cleaned-up-later`, `--observability-layer-optional`, `--doc-move-can-lag` flag.

**§11.4.248 — Flaky-test quarantine + protected-regression-spec gate via `[PROTECTED-SPEC: ATM-NNN]` tag + CODEOWNERS required reviewer (operator decision, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): flaky tests are the SILENT ANTI-BLUFF DECAY vector — every flake conditions the team to ignore red until a real regression is dismissed as "probably flaky, re-run". Two disciplines bind: **(A) QUARANTINE** — mechanical detection (a test-history aggregator flagging a two-consecutive-run-different-verdict or a rerun-after-red pattern) moves the flaky test to `tests/quarantine/` (path per §11.4.35), opens a tracked §11.4.197 stabilisation deadline, and the blocking suite runs WITHOUT the quarantined test so green means green; a deadline past due is a §11.4.66 extension or a violation. **(B) PROTECTED-REGRESSION-SPEC** — a regression test codifying a known-broken defect fix (per the §11.4.135 permanent-guard suite) MAY be tagged `# [PROTECTED-SPEC: ATM-NNN]` (or the target language's comment equivalent) referencing the §11.4.54 workable item it guards, marking it a load-bearing guard whose modification requires elevated review; the tag is applied DELIBERATELY per test — blanket-tagging voids the discipline. CODEOWNERS-REQUIRED-REVIEWER GATE: the consuming project's CODEOWNERS declares that modifications to `[PROTECTED-SPEC]`-tagged tests or a `regression/**/*` path scope require a designated required-reviewer approval BEFORE merge, composing §2.1 SSH + §11.4.113 no-force-push so the gate cannot be silently bypassed, with the audit trail in the review thread and commit history. The gate CATCHES the change so a reviewer can distinguish STRENGTHEN (approve) from FIX-DEFECT-IN-TEST (approve after verifying ATM-NNN's defect is not silently re-introduced) from WEAKEN/REMOVE (refuse, or escalate per §11.4.66) — it makes modification VISIBLE, not impossible. Composes §2.1 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.50 (an empty quarantine is the ideal) / §11.4.54 / §11.4.66 / §11.4.113 / §11.4.115 / §11.4.135 / §11.4.197 / §11.4.226 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-248-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.248`) + recommended gates `CM-FLAKY-TEST-QUARANTINE-WIRED` / `CM-PROTECTED-REGRESSION-SPEC-GATE` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.248 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--rerun-until-green`, `--quarantine-permanent`, `--protected-spec-review-optional`, `--codeowners-skip`, `--blanket-protect-everything`, `--tag-without-atm-id` flag.

**§11.4.249 — Producer ≠ oracle ≠ gate ≠ verifier + flight recorder (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): every load-bearing quality mechanism has FOUR distinct roles that MUST be structurally separate — (1) PRODUCER (the code computing/emitting/building the output); (2) ORACLE (the mechanism deciding correctness per §11.4.245); (3) GATE (the seam consuming the oracle's verdict to allow/block/proceed); (4) VERIFIER (the mechanism auditing the gate's decision — the paired §1.1 mutation proving the gate CAN refuse a bad output). Collapsing any two produces a specific bluff class: producer=oracle (the §11.4.245 oracle-agrees-with-code failure); oracle=gate (an oracle that cannot say "cannot decide" defaults to allow — the §11.4.201(6) FALSE-NULL); gate=verifier (a self-auditing gate — the §11.4.142 self-review anti-pattern applied). FLIGHT RECORDER: a mechanism CAPTURING producer inputs + oracle verdict + gate decision + verifier audit as a durable, replayable artifact (a structured JSONL event stream per §11.4.116, content-addressed per §11.4.207) so the post-hoc question "why did this go through" has evidence rather than a shrug; the recorder is NOT the gate — a gate reading its own recorder to decide collapses gate + verifier + recorder. DETECTABLE COLLAPSES the §11.4.194 reviewer scans for: (1) producer=oracle (the test's assertion calls the code being tested); (2) oracle=gate (no "cannot decide" branch, defaults PASS); (3) gate=verifier (the meta-test IS the gate — a mutation making the gate always-pass ALSO makes the meta-test always-pass; the meta-test MUST live OUTSIDE the gate); (4) recorder=decision-substrate (the gate uses recorder contents as a decision input — precedent-based gating is not authorised). Canon records a MATERIAL-THIN note: the four-role taxonomy is generalised from the producer/verifier separation + oracle-problem discussion + gate mechanics, and the FLIGHT RECORDER is derived from the clean-checkout re-run + attached-receipts pattern per §11.4.17. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.107(10) / §11.4.116 / §11.4.142 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 / §11.4.207 / §11.4.240 (the separation PRINCIPLE — §11.4.249 is the four-role taxonomy that instantiates it; the two mutually compose) / §11.4.245 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-249-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.249`) + recommended gates `CM-ROLE-SEPARATION-DECLARED` / `CM-FLIGHT-RECORDER-CAPTURED` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.249 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--collapse-producer-oracle`, `--gate-self-audits`, `--skip-flight-recorder`, `--oracle-defaults-pass`, `--decision-without-recorded-rationale` flag.

**§11.4.250 — Heuristic-tower signals a primitive defect: when N heuristic layers stack to compensate, the primitive is broken (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): a codebase accumulating a TOWER of heuristics all compensating for the same underlying issue (retry-with-backoff + connection-pool-warmup + DNS-cache-flush + health-check-with-startup-grace, all guarding one flaky DB connection) IS the diagnostic — the PRIMITIVE is defective and every layer papers over a manifestation without fixing the cause. RECOGNITION SIGNALS: (i) each layer's justification is "we added this because sometimes X happens" — reactive; (ii) removing any single layer INCREASES failures, yet keeping all of them still shows an occasional residual; (iii) no single layer owns responsibility; (iv) the layers were added at different times by different authors per specific incidents; (v) monotone layer-count growth over time — a debugging session for the residual adds layer N+1 rather than fixing 1..N. DISTINCT FROM DEFENSE-IN-DEPTH: defense-in-depth is deliberate, each layer targets a DIFFERENT failure class, the layers are designed together rather than accreted, and removing one produces a SPECIFIC different failure rather than "the same failure more often". SYSTEMATIC-DEBUGGING RESPONSE at tower depth ≥ 2: (1) STOP adding layers; (2) INVOKE §11.4.102 systematic-debugging on the PRIMITIVE; (3) work the root-cause / pattern / hypothesis / implementation phases; (4) fix the primitive; (5) REMOVE the layers one at a time, verifying each removal with §11.4.5 captured evidence; (6) commit each removal per §11.4.124 with git-history evidence citing the primitive fix. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.102 / §11.4.124 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 (an outer layer masks the §11.4.201(6) false-null) / §11.4.226 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-250-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.250`) + recommended gate `CM-HEURISTIC-TOWER-TRIGGERS-PRIMITIVE-DEBUG` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.250 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--add-another-heuristic-layer`, `--skip-primitive-debug`, `--tower-is-defense-in-depth-without-justification`, `--reactive-mitigation-OK`, `--layer-per-incident` flag.

**§11.4.251 — Byte-identical-fork prohibition + role-as-data-pack (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): when a project's needs would be met by TWO NEAR-IDENTICAL CODE COPIES differing only in configuration / data / role-specific parameters (prod + staging with hardcoded hostnames; reader + writer with different flag values; web + mobile with different feature flags), those copies MUST NOT be maintained as parallel forks — ONE code copy is retained and the role-specific values are extracted into a DATA PACK (role-config file / env-var set / capability descriptor / feature-flag bundle) that the one code consumes to instantiate as either role; canon's remedy phrasing: "roles become data-only packs you reference, never repos you fork". DETECTION: a reviewer or mechanical scan finds a byte-identical fork when `diff` shows every difference is a configuration value (hostname/port/flag/label/env/credential-ref) rather than behavioural, a common git-history ancestor exists, and byte identity exceeds the project's §11.4.35 threshold (canon's illustrative figure: > 90%). EXTRACTION: (1) IDENTIFY the configuration surface; (2) EXTRACT it to a per-role data pack; (3) REFACTOR the single code copy to consume it at instantiation (constructor injection / factory / config-loader-at-startup); (4) DELETE one fork; (5) VERIFY per §11.4.5 that both role instantiations produce the same observable behaviour their forks previously produced (§11.4.243 characterization before refactor); (6) commit the extraction per §11.4.124 with git-history evidence. LEGITIMATE FORKS: a temporary migration (tracked §11.4.197 end date), an experimental / A-B fork (bounded end date), or genuinely divergent behaviour (copies differing in algorithm / protocol / data model, not merely config). Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.28 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.124 / §11.4.177 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.197 / §11.4.243 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-251-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.251`) + recommended gate `CM-NO-BYTE-IDENTICAL-FORK` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.251 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--fork-for-role-OK`, `--skip-data-pack-extraction`, `--copy-and-modify-config`, `--duplicate-code-per-environment`, `--near-identical-forks-are-fine` flag.

**§11.4.252 — Fail-closed-on-dangerous-combination for mutating / credential surfaces (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): every code path COMBINING ≥ 2 dangerous capabilities MUST FAIL CLOSED — refusing the operation unless every capability's precondition is verifiably satisfied — and MUST NEVER fail open on ambiguous, missing or unresolvable input; this is the security-engineering generalisation of §11.4.201 guard-honesty applied where a false negative's blast radius dwarfs the operator inconvenience of a false positive. DANGEROUS-COMBINATION TAXONOMY (canon records this 6-class set as a §11.4.17 closed-set formalisation of the fail-closed pattern's shape, MATERIAL-THIN rather than a literal per-class enumeration): (1) MUTATION of a shared resource (DB / filesystem / container state / config); (2) UNTRUSTED INPUT (user string / network bytes / external env var / unverified file); (3) CREDENTIAL ACCESS (secret read / authenticated API call / signing-key use); (4) EXTERNAL SIDE EFFECT (email / webhook / MQ publish / payment endpoint); (5) SHELL / EXEC (subshell / binary with user-influenced args / template eval over user values); (6) IRREVERSIBLE (delete / drop / truncate — force-push already being absolutely forbidden per §11.4.113). Any path combining ≥ 2 is a dangerous combination and MUST fail closed. THE INVARIANT: (1) VERIFY every precondition (authorization + input shape + credential presence + target identity); (2) REFUSE when ANY is unverifiable, with an ERROR EXIT naming the specific unresolved precondition; (3) EMIT captured evidence of the refusal per §11.4.5; (4) NEVER default to allow / proceed / retry-with-relaxed on ambiguity — the §11.4.101 conservative-safe default is REFUSE. FAIL-OPEN ANTI-PATTERNS refused by name: `catch { /* ignore */ }`, `credential = credential || default`, `if (!valid) { log("warn"); return proceed(input); }`, `target = user_input || default_target`, and an unbounded `retry { dangerous(); }`. Distinct from graceful degradation (a cache miss returning a cache-miss response is legitimate and is not a dangerous combination). Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.10 (the credentials specialisation) / §11.4.66 / §11.4.101 / §11.4.113 (its maximal instantiation) / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-252-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.252`) + recommended gate `CM-DANGEROUS-COMBINATION-FAIL-CLOSED` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.252 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--fail-open-on-error`, `--swallow-exception`, `--default-missing-credential`, `--proceed-on-ambiguous-input`, `--retry-until-succeeds` flag.

**§11.4.253 — Idempotency under retry + DB-level durable uniqueness guard (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): every retryable operation (network calls with auto-retry, at-least-once MQ consumers, cron jobs with overlapping schedules, HTTP endpoints with client retry, DB transactions with retry-on-serialisation-failure, deploy pipelines with rerun capability) MUST BE IDEMPOTENT — N executions produce the same observable end state as one — and idempotency MUST be ENFORCED at the LOWEST LAYER where "same end state" is durably provable, typically a DATABASE UNIQUENESS CONSTRAINT on a caller-supplied idempotency key, NEVER only an application-layer check-then-insert (which races). This prevents the DOUBLE-CHARGE / DOUBLE-EMAIL / DOUBLE-DELETE / DOUBLE-EFFECT class. IDEMPOTENCY-KEY DISCIPLINE: every retryable operation accepts (or generates and passes forward) a unique identifier naming THIS logical operation; the persistence layer carries a UNIQUE INDEX / CONSTRAINT on the key column; a second attempt is REFUSED by the DB at the durable level rather than by application logic reading-then-writing; the caller catches that refusal as "duplicate — already succeeded" and treats it as SUCCESS. ANTI-PATTERNS: (i) check-then-insert without a unique index; (ii) an app-level dedup table read outside the spanning transaction; (iii) a key scoped to one app instance (in-memory, not shared); (iv) the effect persisted BEFORE the key (a crash between them double-effects on retry); (v) no key at all plus "the effect is 'set X to V' so redoing is fine" — safe for pure sets, NOT for INSERTs, MQ emissions or external-system side effects; (vi) an expiring idempotency key, which converts the operation to time-bounded-idempotent and is a §11.4.6 mis-labelling if the caller expects unbounded. CHAOS VERIFICATION per §11.4.85: (1) SIMULTANEOUS RETRIES (same key from two concurrent callers); (2) MID-OPERATION CRASHES (kill the receiver between "check exists" and "insert effect"); (3) RETRY AFTER APPARENT SUCCESS (the caller believes it failed, the receiver persisted, and the retry MUST recognise the duplicate). For external side effects the key MUST pass through to the external system; where the external system does not support one, the operation is NOT fully idempotent and MUST be labelled honestly per §11.4.6. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.85 / §11.4.201 / §11.4.252 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-253-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.253`) + recommended gates `CM-IDEMPOTENCY-DB-LEVEL-UNIQUE-GUARD` / `CM-IDEMPOTENCY-CHAOS-VERIFIED` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.253 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--check-then-insert-OK`, `--application-dedup-suffices`, `--skip-idempotency-chaos-test`, `--claim-idempotent-without-key`, `--expire-idempotency-marker-silently` flag.

**§11.4.254 — Boot-time invariant assertion + capability matrix (research-derived, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): every service / process / long-running executable MUST assert its BOOT-TIME INVARIANTS at startup — required env vars present and valid; required config files parseable with a matching declared schema version; required dependencies reachable; required credentials present and unexpired; required schema migrations applied; required feature-flag states declared; required host resources present (disk / memory / thread limits per §12.12 / port availability) — and MUST FAIL FAST AND LOUD if any is violated, BEFORE accepting a request or performing work. INVARIANT CLASSES: (i) environment, (ii) configuration, (iii) dependencies, (iv) credentials, (v) schema, (vi) capabilities, (vii) host. FAIL-FAST-LOUD: (1) EMIT a structured error to stderr plus a captured log naming the specific failed invariant; (2) EXIT non-zero (traditionally 78 EX_CONFIG for configuration; the consumer supplies its code per §11.4.35); (3) DO NOT accept requests, begin the main event loop, or bind the listening port. A service that boots partially and serves requests-that-will-fail is WORSE than one refusing to start — a partial boot masquerades as healthy long enough for a load balancer to route traffic to it; this is the dual of §11.4.252's fail-closed-on-dangerous-combination. CAPABILITY MATRIX: the consumer's `docs/services/CAPABILITY_MATRIX.md` (per §11.4.35) declares, per service, (i) its boot-time invariants, (ii) the runtime capabilities it OFFERS, (iii) the runtime capabilities it REQUIRES from other services; it is regenerated and verified on every commit per §11.4.106 docs-chain + §11.4.86 fingerprint. An undeclared endpoint emitted is a violation, and a silent upstream dependency not declared is a violation (it is invisible to §11.4.244 contract testing). Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.86 / §11.4.106 / §11.4.108 Layer 3 (boot IS the runtime-on-clean-target check) / §11.4.128 / §11.4.244 / §11.4.252 / §12.12 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-254-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.254`) + recommended gates `CM-BOOT-TIME-INVARIANT-ASSERTED` / `CM-CAPABILITY-MATRIX-PRESENT-AND-IN-SYNC` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.254 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--skip-boot-time-assertion`, `--boot-partial-OK`, `--default-missing-env-silently`, `--capability-matrix-optional`, `--service-with-undeclared-dependency-OK` flag.

**§11.4.255 — Mandatory HelixTranslate canonical translation pipeline (User mandate, 2026-06-25).** Cascade reference (short form): ALL translations of ANY content — UI strings, documents, articles, CVs, READMEs, marketing copy, in-app messages, release notes, legal text, ANY human-language string rendered in a non-source language — MUST be produced by **HelixTranslate** (`git@github.com:HelixDevelopment/HelixTranslate.git`) driven through the canonical universal translation pipeline shipped in the constitution submodule at `scripts/translation/` (`translate-pipeline.sh` + `render-articles.sh`); a hard, non-negotiable constraint. FORBIDDEN and constituting a violation: (a) HAND-TRANSLATION — no human-authored, manually edited or "good enough" hand-written translation of any content, ever; the engine is the single source of every translated string; (b) OTHER ENGINES — no Google Translate, DeepL, raw ChatGPT/Claude prompting, OS translation APIs, library i18n auto-translators, or any engine other than HelixTranslate's `unified-translator`; there is exactly one engine; (c) SILENT FALLBACK — no quiet substitution of the source string, no empty/partial output passed off as a translation, no swallowed engine error, no absence-of-error PASS (§11.4/§11.4.1); on exhaustion of all configured providers the pipeline MUST FAIL LOUD (non-zero exit, destination NOT written), a missing translation being a hard error never a degraded-but-shipped string, while provider-to-provider failover WITHIN HelixTranslate (with logged, evidenced retries) is NOT a silent fallback and is permitted. MANDATORY VALIDATION: every generated translation is validated for accuracy with real captured evidence per §11.4.5/§11.4.69/§11.4.107 — the per-file run log (provider used, byte counts, retries, success/failure) IS the evidence artifact, and an unvalidated or unevidenced translation is treated as ABSENT. EXPLICIT PROVIDER ROUTING: routing MUST be explicit and recorded, never implicit — the canonical routing is provider `groq` / model `llama-3.3-70b-versatile` with documented failover to provider `mistral` / model `mistral-large-latest`, the actual producing provider recorded per file, any deviation stated explicitly in configuration and logged. The engine binary is supplied by the consuming project via `HELIX_TRANSLATE_BIN`. Composes §11.4 / §11.4.1 / §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.7 / §11.4.69 / §11.4.107. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-255-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.255`) + recommended gate `CM-MANDATORY-HELIXTRANSLATE-PIPELINE` + paired §1.1 meta-test mutation (gate-code = separate work item). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.255 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--allow-hand-translation`, `--other-engine-OK`, `--skip-translation-validation`, `--silent-fallback` flag.

**§11.4.256 — Mandatory independent per-language translation review (User mandate, 2026-06-25).** Cascade reference (short form): every translated artifact produced under §11.4.255 MUST, before it is shipped / committed / deployed, be INDEPENDENTLY REVIEWED for quality by a per-language review agent — a hard, non-negotiable gate that composes with and NEVER replaces the §11.4.255 generation mandate. Requirements: (a) INDEPENDENCE — the reviewer MUST be a different model/provider than the one that produced the translation (e.g. translator `mistral/mistral-large-latest` → reviewer `groq/llama-3.3-70b-versatile`, or any distinct verified model); a model reviewing its own output is NOT an independent review and is forbidden; (b) PER-LANGUAGE SPECIALIZATION — the reviewer acts as a NATIVE speaker / linguistic reviewer of the SPECIFIC target language, evaluating that language's grammar, orthography and correct script (Cyrillic for ru/be/kk, Arabic for ar/fa, Han/Kana/Hangul/Devanagari for zh/ja/ko/hi, Latin for sr/de/es/fr/tr, etc.); (c) FULL CRITERIA — each review judges accuracy/fidelity, fluency/naturalness, completeness (nothing omitted or invented), correct script and orthography, and the absence of untranslated source leftovers (proper nouns / URLs / code excepted); (d) STRICT MACHINE-READABLE VERDICT + EVIDENCE — a structured PASS/FAIL verdict with per-criterion scores and an issues list, persisted as a physical evidence artifact (§11.4.5/§11.4.69/§11.4.107); a FAIL — or an ERROR / unavailable-reviewer — BLOCKS the content and only a real PASS may ship, with no metadata-only / absence-of-error PASS (§11.4/§11.4.1) and no bluff (§11.4.6); (e) REMEDIATION LOOP — a FAIL routes the artifact back through the §11.4.255 pipeline (re-translate / multipass-polish) and is re-reviewed, iterating until PASS, NEVER hand-patched to pass. Canonical tooling: `scripts/translation/review-translations.sh` + `scripts/translation/review_translation.py`, reusable verbatim, with the consuming project supplying its source/translation file pairs and a reviewer provider DISTINCT from its translator. Composes §11.4.255 (the translation it gates) / §11.4 / §11.4.1 / §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.69 / §11.4.107 / §11.4.134. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-256-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.256`) + recommended gate `CM-MANDATORY-TRANSLATION-REVIEW` + paired §1.1 meta-test mutation (gate-code = separate work item). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.256 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--skip-translation-review`, `--self-review-OK`, `--accept-unreviewed-translation` flag.

**§11.4.257 — Comprehensive user-manual + guide + FAQ coverage: every component / service / feature / user-visible workflow ships an operator-usable manual, task-oriented guide, and FAQ, always in sync, no orphan capability (BACKGROUND :: REMINDER :: IMPORTANT operator mandate, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): every consuming project MUST cover the WHOLE project (every component, service, subsystem, binary, container, plugin, feature and user-visible workflow) with three cooperating documentation classes — (a) a complete USER MANUAL per component/service/product, (b) task-oriented GUIDES for every user-facing workflow, (c) properly-created FAQs derived from real operator/QA/end-user questions — always in sync with the shipped code, always exported per §11.4.65, always reachable from README per §11.4.212, always regenerated via Docs-Chain per §11.4.106, and tracked as §11.4.197 workable items when incomplete; a capability that ships without its trio is a violation of §11.4 PASS-bluff severity at the documentation layer (the feature ships to a user who cannot use it because nothing tells them how). ALL hold: **(A)** no orphan capability — every shipped surface maps to ≥1 manual, ≥1 guide per workflow it exposes, and one FAQ entry per real recurring question; an undocumented shipped capability is UNSHIPPABLE regardless of how well the code works. **(B)** THREE DISTINCT CLASSES, none substituting another — a MANUAL is the operator reference (what it is, inputs/outputs/env/config/ports/dependencies, start/stop/status, logs/metrics/health, failure modes and remediation, complete enough to run + supervise + debug without reading source); a GUIDE is workflow-shaped (prerequisites, step-by-step commands, expected output per step, verification checkpoints, rollback path); an FAQ is built from real recurring signal (operator questions, QA reports, end-user tickets, agent-found-defect narratives), NEVER a fabricated marketing FAQ, each entry citing its question-source class + resolution + a link to the owning manual/guide section. **(C)** ALWAYS IN SYNC — §11.4.44 revision header, Docs-Chain bound at the commit / build / constitution-pull write-seams so an edited component with stale docs REFUSES the commit, §11.4.65 export, §11.4.212 reachability; FAQ entries describing now-fixed defects are RETAINED with a "resolved in vN.N.N" note, never silently deleted. **(D)** missing docs are §11.4.197 items, with per-surface docs-status rows in the §11.4.93/§11.4.95 workable-items DB so "what is documented" is a live query. **(E)** HONESTY — a genuine in-flight gap is marked `[DOC-PENDING: <tracker-id>]` per §11.4.223 at both the README entry point and the component Status doc, NEVER silently omitted and NEVER faked with a placeholder paragraph that says nothing (a fake doc is a §11.4 PASS-bluff — the operator reads it, learns nothing, and believes they read the doc). Honest boundary (§11.4.6): the anchor mandates the docs EXIST, are IN SYNC and are REACHABLE; it does NOT verify semantic correctness (that stays §11.4.99 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.238), and an internal implementation detail with no user-facing exposure carries no user-manual obligation. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-257-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.257`) + recommended gates `CM-USER-MANUAL-PER-COMPONENT` / `CM-GUIDE-PER-USER-WORKFLOW` / `CM-FAQ-FROM-REAL-QUESTIONS` + paired §1.1 meta-test mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.257 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--skip-user-manual`, `--guide-later`, `--faq-optional`, `--capability-without-docs-OK`, `--fake-doc-placeholder-OK`, `--source-of-truth-is-code-comments` flag.

**§11.4.258 — Architectural + data-flow + state-machine + sequence-diagram coverage: every project ships accurate, exported, machine-derivable-or-honestly-authored diagrams embedded into the docs that describe the system (BACKGROUND :: REMINDER :: IMPORTANT operator mandate, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): every consuming project MUST cover the WHOLE project with properly created graphs / diagrams / schemes across FOUR canonical classes — (a) SYSTEM ARCHITECTURE (services, containers, ports, dependencies, deployment topology, kept true to the actual compose file / deployment manifest / running system); (b) DATA FLOW (how a user request, event, credential, artifact or evidence traverses source → transformation → sink, each edge annotated with format + protocol + trust boundary); (c) STATE MACHINE (the lifecycle of every stateful entity — workable items §11.4.15, long-ops §11.4.232, orchestrator sessions §11.4.187, agents §11.4.147, deploy artifacts §11.4.108, builds — with transitions labelled by triggering event and gating guard); (d) SEQUENCE (one per critical user workflow and per critical cross-component protocol, with actors + messages + return paths + FAILURE branches, not only the happy path). ALL hold: **(B)** INCORPORATION, never orphan — each diagram lives at its point of use (architecture in README + `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`; per-service data-flow embedded in that service's §11.4.257 manual; state-machine embedded in the doc that owns the state; sequence in the guide for the workflow it depicts); a `docs/diagrams/` gallery may exist as an INDEX, but the load-bearing appearance is EMBEDDED where the reader needs it (§11.4.212). **(C)** OPEN + DIFFABLE — sources in diffable text (Mermaid / PlantUML / D2 / diffable SVG), NEVER a proprietary-tool-only master (§11.4.220); rendered outputs committed alongside sources; renderers §11.4.201-probed before use, an absent tool yielding an honest §11.4.3 SKIP-with-reason and never a fake output (§11.4.222). **(D)** ACCURACY VERIFICATION — every diagram rendered non-blank, non-degenerate and accurate, with a probe render (a Mermaid parse-check, an SVG geometry check, a §11.4.107(10) golden-good vs golden-bad-with-drift fixture for derived diagrams); a diagram whose depicted service list, port map or state set diverges from its SSoT is caught by a §11.4.86 fingerprint diff plus a stale-diagram audit at pre-build, and Docs-Chain (§11.4.106) refuses a commit that changes an SSoT without the mapped diagram refresh. **(E)** DERIVED-WHERE-POSSIBLE — prefer generated diagrams (compose-file-driven architecture, schema-driven state machine, route-table-driven sequence); hand-drawn diagrams are LABELED as such with an authored date and reconciled against the SSoT each release round. A rendered blank / mislabelled / truncated diagram is a §11.4/§11.4.1 bluff at the visual-doc layer. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): the anchor does NOT claim every class applies to every project — an internal library with no runtime deployment ships its module-dependency diagram in the ARCHITECTURE class's place. §11.4.258 STRENGTHENS §11.4.218 and §11.4.219 from design-side coverage into whole-project documentation-side diagram coverage. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-258-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.258`) + recommended gates `CM-ARCHITECTURE-DIAGRAM-PRESENT-ACCURATE` / `CM-DATAFLOW-DIAGRAM-PER-SERVICE` / `CM-STATEMACHINE-DIAGRAM-PER-STATEFUL-ENTITY` / `CM-SEQUENCE-DIAGRAM-PER-CRITICAL-WORKFLOW` / `CM-DIAGRAM-EMBEDDED-NOT-ORPHAN` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.258 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--skip-architecture-diagram`, `--diagram-in-orphan-directory`, `--proprietary-master-only`, `--stale-diagram-OK`, `--dataflow-implied-from-code`, `--sequence-later` flag.

**§11.4.259 — README quality-status badge row: at the top of every project README, a comprehensive row of quality badges with a closed green→amber→red vocabulary, each backed by machine-derived source data, illustrating quality and production-readiness in one glance (BACKGROUND :: REMINDER :: IMPORTANT operator mandate, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): every consuming project MUST render, at the TOP of the main README (immediately below the H1, above the introduction — the reader's first visual signal), a comprehensive BADGE ROW carrying every applicable quality signal with a CLOSED colour vocabulary, plus a production-readiness gauge summarising how close the project is to full production deployment, with EVERY badge backed by MACHINE-DERIVED source data; a README lacking the row, or carrying badges whose colours are fabricated / stale / not backed by real source data, is a violation of §11.4 PASS-bluff severity at the reader-first-impression layer.
